# Changelog
- 2026-11-22 (v1144): 🏙️ City visuals (city.js): realistic colors (ground, park, sea, sand + wet band, asphalt, sidewalks, trees, roof palettes); detailed roofs `hipRoof` / `flatRoof` (shaded faces, chimneys, solar panels, AC units, lift room, water tank) for filler + main buildings; shop awnings + sign plates; billboards shrunk 112×28 → 58×17 (night glow smaller); 💬 proximity bubbles `drawBubbles` for billboards / buildings / shops / homes (nearest 4); 🛣️ street names (`RN`, `RS`, `drawStreetNames`). EN keys Caddesi/Sokak/Bulvarı/Meydanı. Filler layout may shift slightly (bills smaller). sw v1144.
- 2026-11-22 (v1143): 🧬 Body consistency: traits.js `BODY_PAIRS` → two-way rules `b_*` (Petite ✗ Boob 90/95 · Butt L/XL · Thick Thighs · Long legs · Wide waist; Hourglass ✗ Boob 70 · Butt XS/S · Wide waist; Pear ✗ Butt XS/S · Slim legs; Athletic/Toned ✗ Wide waist; Muscular ✗ Tiny waist · Very Short; Thick Thighs ✗ Butt XS; Long legs ✗ Very Short; Tiny waist ✗ Weight Average; Wide waist ✗ Butt XS; Boob 95 ✗ Butt XS; Boob 70 ✗ Butt XL); implants skip `w_*` and `b_*` on Boob/Butt (`impSkip`). 🌍 Origin: app.js `ORIGIN_SIZE` (height / boob / butt weights per region, merged into `ORIGIN_LOOK`) used by 🎲 random (`randomize` weights now index `g.items`) and trait-less height (`cmBody`, `ccHeight` via `cmLookV`); `crBlocked` blocks Skin Tone values with weight 0 in the region (text `🌍 <region>`); Create `#cCity` onchange runs `crFix(d, true)`. sw v1143.
- 2026-11-22 (v1142): 🌸 Create consistency rules (traits.js `rules`, ids `v_*`, two-way): Married / Divorced / Kocasıyla Yaşıyor / 🔘 evli / hotwife / hamile ✗ Experience: Virgo + 🔘 Bakire; Virgo / Bakire ✗ those + Pussy Size Relaxed–Very Loose + Anal Size Loose–Very Loose; Bakire also ✗ Experience Medium+. app.js Create `cfBox` toggle now refuses a blocked `f:` field (📏 notify). 🔐 Onboarding: 🎬 intro tour (`TOUR` / `drawTour` / `tourGo`) removed, "Hazırsın" card buttons → 🏙️ `#/sehir` + 🏠 `#/home` (EN key 'siteler, haberler, ilanlar'). sw v1142.
- 2026-11-22 (v1141): 💍 Street talk matches the profile: app.js `ccRel` now returns `wed` (Evli switch or Relationship Married, strangers too), `rel` (Single / Has Boyfriend / Married / Divorced), `live` (`liveOf`); city.js talk stores `T.rel` / `T.live`, `tAns` 'sevgili' answers per rel (married / boyfriend / divorced / single), 'nereli' (+ "kiminle yaşıyorsun") says who she lives with; LAF_WED for strangers too. 🚗 Own car: `CAR_ME` pink SUV parked by your home (`myCar`, saved `car` [x, y, sa] + `dr`), side button `data-ct="car"` (🚗 / 🅿️, `carBtn`) and tap on the parked car → `openMyCar`; `enterCar` (walk to it first via `me.toCar` if >140) / `exitCar` (park at curb, step to sidewalk); driving: `walkTo` routes on roads (`route(...,1)` + lane offset), `me.driveTo` → park + walk the rest for building / person targets, hold-to-drive 0.85× of 210, `me.sa` steering, drawn with `drawCar` + `drawPax` (you + riders), others slow behind you (`drvs`); chase / talk / join group / enter building auto-park; no taxi button / no "stop you" while driving. Escort: riders `a.inCar` (board within 220 on enter, or come to the car within 60), follow inside, `CAR_WITH` lines, home arrival radius 170 while driving → park + meet. News + book + EN keys. Stub test: enter → tap-drive → park saved, no errors. sw v1141.
- 2026-11-22 (v1140): 🏙️ City: 👫 after a chat she's beside you — `meetWith(L)` before every `api.meet` / `meetGroup` / `ccNurse` from the city saves `wth` ids + `wthAt` in `amor-stories:city`; on mount (≤3 h) those agents stand beside you as escort phase `with` (follow only, `WITH_HI` / `WITH_WALK`; card 🏡 Eve davet et → `startEscort` of the whole pair, 👋 Vedalaş → `stopEscort` clears `wth`). 📱 City phone: `.ct-side` 📱 button (`data-ct="phone"`, unread `.ct-dot`) → `openPhone` lists `api.chats()` = app.js `ccChats` (threads with `t.city` (set in `ccMeet`) or title CarmenCity + groups `gsrc: 'city'`, newest first, `unread`), rows link `#/chat/<id>`. city.css `.ct-phone` / `.ct-dot` / `.ct-new`. News + book + EN keys. Headless stub test: phone lists rows, dot shows, restore runs clean. sw v1140.
- 2026-11-22 (v1139): 🗣️ Street talk (city.js): positive ilgi gains × `TALK_UP` 1.6 (say + choose); 💡 suggestion chips `.ct-sug` / `data-sg` above the input (`SUG` q / ilt / esp / dav / ces / ans; rebuilt per new log line `T.sugAt`; tap fills the input). 🏡 Walk home together: `startEscort(L)` (home card invite outside, card 🏡 Eve davet et `data-home` for known near chars, talk win 🏡 Eve götür `data-gohome`) → phase `come` (they route to you at 2.4×, >900 away jump near camera) → `go` (auto `walkTo(homeDoor())`, they follow behind / beside via `stepTo`, `ESC_HI` / `ESC_WALK` bubbles) → within 70 of door: `api.meet` (`ESC_DOOR` opener, known = !str) or `meetGroup`; `a.esc` excluded from routines / laf / groups; cancel `data-escx` → `stopEscort`. Inside → old instant invite. city.css `.ct-sg`. News + book + EN keys. Stub-api mount in headless Edge: no errors. sw v1139.

News item + book lines + EN keys. sw v1138.

Dots only if no photo pool. Book line in app.js. sw v1137.
- 2026-11-22 (v1136): 🏙️ City (city.js): 👥 join group chats — member card `data-gjoin` → `joinAsk` (g.joined, walk there `me.joinG` or join at <80) → `joinGroup` = `startTalk(a)` + `talk.g`, others greet `GJ_HI`, chime in 55% after each reply `grpChime` (`GJ_OK` / `GJ_BAD`), log entries carry speaker name (`.ct-bn`), main + chimes also bubble on the map; endTalk → `grpEnd`; joined group waits 25 s; holding a member no longer breaks a group. 🏖️ Beach widened: `beach` x 700–2320, sand stroke 390, trees coastDist ≥195, two umbrella rows, `areaPt` sahil depth 30–160, hit test coastDist <200; sunbathers `sunb` (towel + lying body + bikini, 08–19) and anonymous standing groups `bgrp` (2–4 dots, `BEACH_CONV` bubbles, `beachStep`, 08–21); `drawLaf` bubble helper `bub`. 🚶 Peds 220 → 520, `ROUTE_N` 10, finished peds >1500 from camera respawn at an off-screen node near the camera (`nearCamNode`). 📰 News item + 📚 Şehir Kitabı lines, EN keys (i18n-en-books.js). sw v1136.
- 2026-11-22 (v1135): 🏙️ City street life (city.js): 📣 `LAF` / `LAF_ST` / `LAF_NIGHT` / `LAF_WED` ~2× lines; ✋ she stops you (`lafStep` 18% on-foot → `a.seek` {t, rp, txt}, `seekStep` re-routes to you every 1.2 s at 2.2× speed, on reach `resume(a)` + `startTalk` with `STOP_L[tier]` opener + LAF_I bonus, gives up after 20 s / inside / taxi); 👥 groups `grpStep` (≤2 groups, 2–3 visible free walkers meet at a sidewalk point, slots r13, take turns with `CONV` lines in grey bubbles, `grpEnd` → `resume`; tapping / talking to a member breaks it); 📱 `phoneStep` (≤2 callers, `PHONE` monologue 3.3 s/line, walk ×0.6). `resume(a)` = continue to `a.to` / `a.k` or stand; `freeA(a)`; `drawLaf` shows newest of laf / suz / chat (`a.chatT` / `a.chatTxt`, grey, 3 s). 📰 HOME_NEWS top item + 📚 Şehir Kitabı (hold-to-walk, wider sidewalks, 👀 Süz, groups, phone, traffic, typed talk, questions both ways, she stops you) in app.js; EN keys in i18n-en-books.js. sw v1135.

- 2026-11-22 (v1133): 🏙️ City (city.js): 👀 Süz now shows her reaction line (`SUZ` by tier, `SUZ_ST` by style 50%, `a.suzTxt`). 🗣️ Ayaküstü konuşma is typed chat (form `.ct-say` / `data-sayf`, `card.onsubmit` → `say(text)`): `TQ` question regexes → `tAns` answers from her data (name / age / job / mood / place / `a.to` / relationship / hobby `TK_HOBI` / home), `TALK_RE` (kaba / cesur / davet / iltifat / espri) → `choose(type, text)` (same ilgi odds), she asks questions too (`HQ`, `T.ask` / `T.asked`, answer → `HR` / `HR_SHORT`), neutral small talk `TK_NEU`, 3 dull in a row `TK_DULL`; shared tail `finish(T, ok, invite, ask)`; no events triggered. Input value + focus kept across `drawTalk` redraws; log shows last 12. 🚗 Car following `carFactor` (slow/stop behind any car ahead within 14 lateral, 2.5 s deadlock pass) for traffic + character cars (`drvs`); 🚶 `separate(dt)` pushes visible walkers apart (grid 16, `CS*.9`, me / held / standing fixed, never into `solid`); sidewalk lanes (`walkPath` `ln`, `laneOf(a)`, ped `o.lane` ±10). city.css `.ct-say`. - 2026-11-21 (v1095): 📱 Real iPhone fix — first boot with empty storage (new device / private tab) took ~68 s of JS in V8 (much longer on iOS JavaScriptCore → stuck on Carmen splash). Cause: `cmNewEvs` / `cmFix` / `klEv` did `allEvents().find(e => low(e.title) === …)` per ad × per title, re-lowercasing every event title with slow `toLocaleLowerCase('tr')`. New helpers next to `tickGet`: `lowTr` (memoized tr-lowercase+trim, Map capped at 50k) and `evByLowTitle(t)` (lowercase title → first event Map, `tickGet('evLowT')` with allEvents' signature — NOT `[allEvents()]`, which is a fresh `.slice()` each call). Used in `cmNewEvs`, `cmFix`, `klEv`, site-wide `low`, `moKey`, `fantKey` (lowercase once instead of per FANT_KEYS entry). Fresh boot JS ~68 s → ~3.7 s → splash gone at ~4.5 s (headless Edge CPU profile).
- 2026-11-21 (v1094): 🩺 `#bootErr` now has a 🧹 "clear cache + reload" button (Cache Storage + SW unregister, data kept) and a 15 s watchdog: if `#splash` is still visible it lists missing `Stories.*` modules, localStorage size, SW state, UA. User report: iPhone 13 / iOS 26.6.1 stuck on the Carmen splash; headless Edge (fresh profile, iPhone UA, simulated 5 MB quota) boots fine → suspect SW cache mixing versions or phone-specific data. Note: a fresh boot already writes ~2.0 M chars to localStorage (~4 MB in Safari's UTF-16 accounting, limit 5 MB).
- 2026-11-21 (v1093): 🩺 Boot error overlay in index.html head (`#bootErr`, `window.__bootErr`): window `error` (capture, incl. failed `<script>` loads) + `unhandledrejection` shown on screen (max 5) — to diagnose the iPhone not opening (still open after v1092 lookbehind fix). No other ES2022+/Safari-incompatible syntax found; total JS payload 14.3 MB (bot-rules.js 4.5 MB, app.js 3.6 MB).
- 2026-11-21 (v1092): 📱 iPhone fix — regex lookbehind `(?<=…)` (unsupported before iOS Safari 16.4) made app.js / i18n.js fail to parse, app blank on older iOS. Replaced in `phCard` / `fnScnHtml` (app.js) and `sentences` (i18n.js) with `replace(/([.!?])\s+/g, '$1\u0001').split('\u0001')`. Rule: never use lookbehind in regexes. 🔄 Fabrika ayarları (Ben → ⚙️ Ayarlar, edit mode only): `#factoryReset` removes every `amor-stories*` localStorage key, sessionStorage, Cache Storage, unregisters service workers, disables `Store.save`, reloads → first-launch (login/onboard).
- 2026-11-20 (v1091): ⚡ Smoother site entry — total JS on entering all 29 sites 4.7 s → 1.6 s (headless): `cmPicks` hashes each item once (decorate-sort, same order), `fnCostumes` memoized per character per task (`fnCostumes0`), `feelPool` / `fantPool` / `gtHw` / `gtBdEvs` via `tickGet` (`gtSig`); `content-visibility: auto` for site card grids (`html[data-site] #view [class*=grid] > *`, `[class*=vrow] > *`, injected in `drawSiteNav`, no stories.css / dark.css change; screenshots identical at page top). Worst entry frames: Carmen 1.4 s → ~0.4 s, Gothic 1.7 s → ~0.2 s, Events 0.8 s → ~0.2 s, News 1.1 s → ~0.2 s, all under the splash.
- 2026-11-20 (v1090): ⚡ Faster save + smooth site ↔ home switches. store.js split storage (`amor-stories:v2:c:<thread>` per chat, `:p:monia`, main has `__split`), only changed keys written (`written`), old single-key format read + chat keys merged by id (`readSplit`); app.js `save()` waits for `requestIdleCallback`. Big save ~95 ms → ~20 ms. `render()`: on site enter/exit the splash is shown first and the page is built ~320 ms later behind it (0 ms if splash already visible), no more `void view.offsetWidth` forced reflow for `page-in`. Tap → first long frame moved from ~20 ms (old page frozen ~250 ms) to ~550 ms (under the opaque splash). See systems/store.md, routing-ui.md.
- 2026-11-20 (v1089): ⚡ Incremental chat rendering — `drawMessages` no longer rebuilds `#msgs` with innerHTML; each item's `itemHtml` string is compared with the previous draw (`box._mc = { ctx, map: key → { h, nodes } }`), unchanged nodes are kept, only new / changed ones are parsed (template) and inserted; foreign nodes (typing indicators) still removed every draw; search (`ctx.query`) and a new ctx do a full draw. Send → reply long tasks ~150–350 ms → ~40–170 ms; result DOM identical to a full render (headless check). See systems/chat.md.
- 2026-11-20 (v1088): ⚡ Performance (measured with headless Edge CPU profile): page switches Home ~5 s → ~0.1–0.2 s, Fans ~10 s → ~0.25 s; chat send freezes 1–3.8 s → ~0.15–0.35 s, typing ~150–200 ms/key → ~2 ms. app.js: `tickGet(k, f, sig)` per-task memo (cleared by `save()` and next task, re-checked by signature) used by `moCats` (+ `catMemo` signature cache, `catOfEv` Map lookup via `catMemo.byId`, catOrder index Map), `allEvents`, `evById` (`feelEvents`), `evFind`, `actEv` / `actOpen` (lowercase-title Maps, `evSig` / `catSig` / `firstBy`); `flavorParts` lowercases titles once. world.js `countryOf` memo per city string. i18n.js `translate` result memo (`memo`, cleared on `add`). See systems/routing-ui.md.
